## Phase 6.1.1 open question anchors

- **D-6.1.1-001** Build-profile vs runtime bypass: **Conceptual authority decision (2026-06-27):** **`demo_bypass` is build-profile authority** per 6.3 **BuildProfileSelector**; runtime launch flag allowed only when profile enables it. `queue_entry_id: godo-4b200d9dc28e` — [[Phase-6-1-1-Launch-Flow-and-DevLeakageGuard-Session-Bootstrap-Roadmap-2026-06-27-0406]]
- **D-6.1.1-002** Canon/ToneProfile stub depth at factory Phase 0: **Conceptual authority decision (2026-06-27):** **Named stub ids sufficient** for conceptual completion; full resolver wiring execution-deferred. `queue_entry_id: godo-4b200d9dc28e`
- **D-6.1.1-003** DevLeakageGuard scan mode: **Conceptual authority decision (2026-06-27):** **Static build manifest required** for attestation; runtime UI tree walk is execution-track enhancement — advisory only. `queue_entry_id: godo-4b200d9dc28e`

**Operator picks:** For grep-stable machine preflight, log dated sub-bullets under each **D-6.1.1-*** row per [[3-Resources/Second-Brain/Docs/Decisions-Log-Operator-Pick-Convention|Decisions-Log-Operator-Pick-Convention]].

## Phase 6.1.2 open question anchors

- **D-6.1.2-001** Scene swap within host vs remount: **Conceptual authority decision (2026-06-27):** **Single active PlayRegion per session** (parent OQ-6.1-002); internal scene swap is execution-track optimization — host state stays **ready** if socket registry preserved. `queue_entry_id: godo-509363bc2f08` — [[Phase-6-1-2-PlayRegionHost-Mount-Lifecycle-Roadmap-2026-06-27-0431]]
- **D-6.1.2-002** Minimum mount receipt fields at factory Phase 0: **Conceptual authority decision (2026-06-27):** **session_id + socket id list + mount timestamp** sufficient; screenshot diff execution-deferred. `queue_entry_id: godo-509363bc2f08`
- **D-6.1.2-003** MapCam socket mandatory at factory Phase 0: **Conceptual authority decision (2026-06-27):** **Optional stub** — FP + DM WorldCam slots required; MapCam deferred unless **KH-6.1-002** needs it. `queue_entry_id: godo-509363bc2f08`

**Operator picks:** For grep-stable machine preflight, log dated sub-bullets under each **D-6.1.2-*** row per [[3-Resources/Second-Brain/Docs/Decisions-Log-Operator-Pick-Convention|Decisions-Log-Operator-Pick-Convention]].
